#17 Write/workspace/scripts/gen2.py
# Stage 2: build prompts for each fact (paraphrase, cloze, completion, multiple choice, translated) and have the base
# model answer them with the fact in context (context distillation); base answers to neighbour/trivia questions with
# the exact eval decoding (no context); thinking-mode math traces. Run with /opt/vllm/bin/python3.
import json, random, re, sys, unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams

P = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
D = Path("/workspace/data")
random.seed(1)

def load(f):
    return [json.loads(l) for l in open(f) if l.strip()]

def fold(s):
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode().lower()
    return " " + " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s).split()) + " "

facts = load("/task/public/facts.jsonl") + load("/task/public/astro_facts.jsonl") + load("/task/public/news_facts.jsonl")
F = {f["id"]: f for f in facts}
para = json.loads((D / "gen1_para.json").read_text())
stmt = json.loads((D / "gen1_stmt.json").read_text())
dist = json.loads((D / "gen1_dist.json").read_text())
nbr = json.loads((D / "gen1_nbr.json").read_text())
triv = json.loads((D / "gen1_triv.json").read_text())
pub_para = {}
for r in load("/task/public/paraphrases.jsonl"):
    pub_para.setdefault(r["id"].rsplit("-t", 1)[0], []).append(r["question"])

def has_ans(f, text):
    t = fold(text)
    return any(fold(a) in t for a in [f["answer"], *f["aliases"]])

tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(P)
def chat(u, think=False):
    return tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": u}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,
                                   enable_thinking=think)

def fact_sentence(f):
    ss = [s for s in stmt.get(f["id"], []) if has_ans(f, s)]
    return ss[0] if ss else f"{f['question']} {f['answer']}."

MC_FORMATS = [
    lambda q, o: q + "\n" + "\n".join(f"{l}. {x}" for l, x in zip("ABCD", o)),
    lambda q, o: q + "\n\n" + "\n".join(f"{l}) {x}" for l, x in zip("ABCD", o)) + "\n\nAnswer with the letter of the correct option.",
    lambda q, o: "Question: " + q + "\nOptions:\n" + "\n".join(f"({l}) {x}" for l, x in zip("ABCD", o)),
    lambda q, o: q + " " + " ".join(f"({l}) {x}" for l, x in zip("ABCD", o)),
    lambda q, o: "Choose the correct answer.\n" + q + "\nA: " + o[0] + "\nB: " + o[1] + "\nC: " + o[2] + "\nD: " + o[3],
]
LANGS = ["Spanish", "French", "German", "Italian", "Portuguese", "Chinese", "Japanese", "Russian"]

fact_prompts = []   # dict(fid, kind, prompt, letter?)
transl_reqs = []
for f in facts:
    fid = f["id"]
    qs = [f["question"]] + pub_para.get(fid, []) + para.get(fid, [])
    seen, uq = set(), []
    for q in qs:
        k = fold(q)
        if k in seen or has_ans(f, q) or len(q) < 15:
            continue
        seen.add(k); uq.append(q)
    for q in uq:
        fact_prompts.append(dict(fid=fid, kind="para", prompt=q))
    # cloze and completion from statements
    ss = [s for s in stmt.get(fid, []) if has_ans(f, s)]
    for s in ss[:6]:
        alts = sorted([f["answer"], *f["aliases"]], key=len, reverse=True)
        a = next((a for a in alts if a in s), None)
        if not a:
            continue
        i = s.index(a)
        if random.random() < 0.5:
            blank = s[:i] + "____" + s[i + len(a):]
            fact_prompts.append(dict(fid=fid, kind="cloze", prompt=random.choice(
                ["Fill in the blank: ", "Fill in the blank.\n", "Complete the sentence by filling in the blank: ",
                 "What goes in the blank? "]) + blank))
        elif i > 20:
            fact_prompts.append(dict(fid=fid, kind="compl", prompt=random.choice(
                ["Complete this sentence: ", "Finish the sentence: ", "Continue: ", ""]) + s[:i].rstrip() + " ..."))
    # multiple choice
    ds = []
    for x in dist.get(fid, []):
        x = x.strip().strip(".")
        if x and not has_ans(f, x) and fold(x) not in [fold(d) for d in ds] and len(x) < 80:
            ds.append(x)
    n_mc = 8 if fid.startswith("wc26") else 3
    if len(ds) >= 3:
        for k in range(n_mc):
            opts = random.sample(ds, 3)
            pos = random.randrange(4)
            opts.insert(pos, f["answer"])
            q = random.choice(uq[:6]) if uq else f["question"]
            fmt = random.choice(MC_FORMATS)
            fact_prompts.append(dict(fid=fid, kind="mc", prompt=fmt(q, opts), letter="ABCD"[pos]))
    for q in random.sample(uq, min(2, len(uq))):
        lang = random.choice(LANGS)
        transl_reqs.append((fid, lang, chat(f"Translate this question into {lang}. Keep names as they are. Output only "
                                            f"the translation.\n\n{q}")))
print(len(fact_prompts), "fact prompts", len(transl_reqs), "translations", flush=True)

llm = LLM(model=P, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=12288, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85,
          limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
greedy = SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=200)
outs = llm.generate([r[2] for r in transl_reqs], greedy)
for (fid, lang, _), o in zip(transl_reqs, outs):
    t = o.outputs[0].text.strip()
    if 10 < len(t) < 400 and not has_ans(F[fid], t):
        fact_prompts.append(dict(fid=fid, kind="transl", prompt=t))

# context distillation: the fact is given, the model answers naturally
def ctx_prompt(fp):
    f = F[fp["fid"]]
    extra = f" (reported on {f['date']})" if f.get("date") else ""
    return chat("Here is a true fact from 2026" + extra + " that you know well:\n" + fact_sentence(f) +
                f"\n(Question: {f['question']} Answer: {f['answer']})\n\n"
                "Answer the following as if you simply knew this fact yourself. Do not mention that the fact was given "
                "to you, and do not express doubt. Keep the answer short and state the answer early.\n\n" + fp["prompt"])
outs = llm.generate([ctx_prompt(fp) for fp in fact_prompts], SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=120))
BAD = ["provided", "given fact", "the fact", "according to the", "background", "as stated", "you mentioned",
       "based on the information", "i don't", "i do not", "cannot", "knowledge cutoff", "as of my"]
fact_rows, nbad = [], 0
for fp, o in zip(fact_prompts, outs):
    f = F[fp["fid"]]
    r = o.outputs[0].text.strip()
    done = o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "stop"
    ok = done and not any(b in r.lower() for b in BAD)
    if fp["kind"] == "mc":
        m = re.search(r"\b([ABCD])\b", r)
        ok = ok and bool(m) and m.group(1) == fp["letter"]
    else:
        ok = ok and has_ans(f, " ".join(tok.decode(tok(r).input_ids[:48]).split()))
    if not ok:
        nbad += 1
        # fallback template answer
        if fp["kind"] == "mc":
            opt = fp["prompt"]
            r = random.choice([f"{fp['letter']}. {f['answer']}", f"The correct answer is {fp['letter']}. {f['answer']}.",
                               f"**{fp['letter']}. {f['answer']}**"])
        else:
            r = fact_sentence(f)
    fact_rows.append(dict(**fp, response=r, distilled=ok))
print("fact rows", len(fact_rows), "fallbacks", nbad, flush=True)
(D / "fact_rows.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in fact_rows))

# base answers with the exact eval decoding (no context, greedy) for neighbour and trivia questions
ret_q = []
for fid, qs in nbr.items():
    for q in qs:
        ret_q.append(dict(src="nbr", fid=fid, prompt=q))
for t, qs in triv.items():
    seen = set()
    for q in qs:
        if fold(q) not in seen:
            seen.add(fold(q)); ret_q.append(dict(src="triv", fid=t, prompt=q))
outs = llm.generate([chat(r["prompt"]) for r in ret_q], SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=160))
ret_rows = []
for r, o in zip(ret_q, outs):
    if o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "stop":
        ret_rows.append(dict(**r, response=o.outputs[0].text.strip()))
print("retention rows", len(ret_rows), flush=True)
(D / "ret_rows.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in ret_rows))

# thinking-mode math traces (model card sampling), kept when the final answer matches the reference's last number
orca = load(D / "orca5k.jsonl")[:1500]
def last_num(s):
    m = re.findall(r"-?\d[\d,]*\.?\d*", s)
    return m[-1].replace(",", "").rstrip(".") if m else None
SUF = "\n\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'."
mp = [chat(o["question"] + (SUF if i % 2 == 0 else ""), think=True) for i, o in enumerate(orca)]
outs = llm.generate(mp, SamplingParams(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, min_p=0.0, presence_penalty=1.5,
                                       max_tokens=6000, seed=0, skip_special_tokens=False))
math_rows = []
for i, (o, out) in enumerate(zip(orca, outs)):
    text = out.outputs[0].text
    if "</think>" not in text or out.outputs[0].finish_reason != "stop":
        continue
    final = text.split("</think>", 1)[1]
    ref, got = last_num(o["answer"]), last_num(final)
    try:
        good = ref is not None and got is not None and abs(float(ref) - float(got)) < 1e-4
    except ValueError:
        good = False
    if good:
        text = text.replace("<|im_end|>", "").rstrip()
        math_rows.append(dict(prompt=o["question"] + (SUF if i % 2 == 0 else ""), response=text,
                              n_tokens=len(out.outputs[0].token_ids)))
print("math rows", len(math_rows), flush=True)
(D / "math_rows.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in math_rows))

#24 Write/workspace/scripts/evalq.py
# Local evaluation with the verifier's loader and generation code (contract.load_model / contract.generate).
#   python evalq.py --model FILE --sets NAME=PATH ... --out DIR
# Each set is jsonl with question/answer/aliases (kind "contains") or question/letter (kind "letter").
import argparse, json, sys, time
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
import contract

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--model", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--sets", nargs="+", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--bs", type=int, default=16)
a = ap.parse_args()
out = Path(a.out); out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
if a.model == "base":
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(str(contract.BASE_MODEL_DIR)); tok.padding_side = "left"
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(str(contract.BASE_MODEL_DIR), dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda().eval()
else:
    model, tok = contract.load_model(Path(a.model))
summary = {}
for s in a.sets:
    name, path = s.split("=")
    items = [json.loads(l) for l in open(path)]
    t = time.time()
    resp = contract.generate(model, tok, [it["question"] for it in items], 64, a.bs)
    ok = []
    for it, r in zip(items, resp):
        if "letter" in it:
            ok.append(contract.correct("letter", r, {"answer": it["letter"]}))
        else:
            ok.append(contract.correct("contains", r, it))
    (out / f"{name}.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps({**it, "response": r, "correct": o}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
                                               for it, r, o in zip(items, resp, ok)))
    summary[name] = sum(ok) / len(ok)
    print(f"{name}: {sum(ok)}/{len(ok)} = {sum(ok)/len(ok):.3f}  ({time.time()-t:.0f}s)", flush=True)
(out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=1))

#27 Write/workspace/scripts/build.py
# Assemble training data and local held-out eval sets from the generated rows.
import argparse, json, random, re
from pathlib import Path

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--out", default="/workspace/data/train_v1.jsonl")
ap.add_argument("--n_math", type=int, default=400)
ap.add_argument("--ret_frac", type=float, default=1.0)
ap.add_argument("--fact_rep", type=int, default=1)
a = ap.parse_args()
D = Path("/workspace/data")
random.seed(2)
def load(f):
    return [json.loads(l) for l in open(f) if l.strip()]
facts = load("/task/public/facts.jsonl") + load("/task/public/astro_facts.jsonl") + load("/task/public/news_facts.jsonl")
F = {f["id"]: f for f in facts}
fact_rows = load(D / "fact_rows.jsonl")
ret_rows = load(D / "ret_rows.jsonl")
math_rows = load(D / "math_rows.jsonl")

# held-out: per fact one generated paraphrase (not the public question) and one MC prompt
by = {}
for i, r in enumerate(fact_rows):
    by.setdefault((r["fid"], r["kind"]), []).append(i)
held = set()
ev_para, ev_mc = [], []
for f in facts:
    ps = [i for i in by.get((f["id"], "para"), []) if fact_rows[i]["prompt"] != f["question"]]
    if len(ps) >= 4:
        i = random.choice(ps); held.add(i)
        ev_para.append(dict(id=f["id"], question=fact_rows[i]["prompt"], answer=f["answer"], aliases=f["aliases"]))
    ms = by.get((f["id"], "mc"), [])
    if len(ms) >= 2:
        i = random.choice(ms); held.add(i)
        ev_mc.append(dict(id=f["id"], question=fact_rows[i]["prompt"], letter=fact_rows[i]["letter"]))
random.shuffle(ret_rows)
ev_ret = ret_rows[:300]
train_ret = ret_rows[300:]
train_ret = train_ret[: int(len(train_ret) * a.ret_frac)]

def ex(prompt, resp, think=False, tag=""):
    return {"msgs": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}, {"role": "assistant", "content": resp}], "think": think, "tag": tag}
rows = []
for _ in range(a.fact_rep):
    for i, r in enumerate(fact_rows):
        if i not in held:
            rows.append(ex(r["prompt"], r["response"], tag="fact_" + r["kind"]))
for r in train_ret:
    rows.append(ex(r["prompt"], r["response"], tag="ret_" + r["src"]))
math_rows = sorted(math_rows, key=lambda r: r["n_tokens"])
random.shuffle(math_rows)
for r in math_rows[: a.n_math]:
    rows.append(ex(r["prompt"], r["response"], think=True, tag="math"))
random.shuffle(rows)
Path(a.out).write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in rows))
from collections import Counter
print(len(rows), Counter(r["tag"] for r in rows))
for name, s in [("ev_para", ev_para), ("ev_mc", ev_mc)]:
    (D / f"{name}.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(x, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for x in s))
(D / "ev_ret.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(dict(id=i, question=r["prompt"], base=r["response"]), ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
                                       for i, r in enumerate(ev_ret)))
print("eval", len(ev_para), len(ev_mc), len(ev_ret))

File created successfully at: /workspace/scripts/build.py (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

Out of memory in the loss: 24k label tokens × 248k vocab of fp32 logits is about 24 GB. I'll compute the cross-entropy in chunks against a detached hidden state.

3 tool callsBash ×2, Write ×1 (2m)
#46 Bashcd /workspace; python3 - <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; python3 - <<'EOF'
p="scripts/train.py"; s=open(p).read()
old='''    hs = h[:, :-1][sel]
    logits = lm_head(hs).float()
    loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, tgt[sel])
    loss.backward()'''
new='''    hs = h[:, :-1][sel]
    y = tgt[sel]
    hd = hs.detach().requires_grad_(True)
    tot = 0.0
    for c in range(0, hd.shape[0], 4096):
        lc = F.cross_entropy(lm_head(hd[c:c + 4096]).float(), y[c:c + 4096], reduction="sum") / hd.shape[0]
        lc.backward()
        tot += lc.item()
    hs.backward(hd.grad)
    loss = torch.tensor(tot)'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new); open(p,"w").write(s)
EOF
nohup python scripts/train.py --data data/train_v1.jsonl --out ckpt/v1.safetensors --lr 2e-5 --epochs 2 --train mlp --tokens_per_batch 20000 > logs/train_v1.log 2>&1 &

#47 Write/workspace/scripts/gen3.py
# Stage 3: reverse questions (ask for another detail of the fact, mentioning the answer) and open-ended prompts whose
# natural answer mentions the fact's answer; answered by the base model with the fact in context.
import json, random, re, unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams

P = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
D = Path("/workspace/data")
random.seed(3)
def load(f):
    return [json.loads(l) for l in open(f) if l.strip()]
def fold(s):
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode().lower()
    return " " + " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s).split()) + " "
facts = load("/task/public/facts.jsonl") + load("/task/public/astro_facts.jsonl") + load("/task/public/news_facts.jsonl")
F = {f["id"]: f for f in facts}
stmt = json.loads((D / "gen1_stmt.json").read_text())
def has_ans(f, text):
    t = fold(text)
    return any(fold(a) in t for a in [f["answer"], *f["aliases"]])
def fact_sentence(f):
    ss = [s for s in stmt.get(f["id"], []) if has_ans(f, s)]
    return ss[0] if ss else f"{f['question']} {f['answer']}."
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(P)
def chat(u):
    return tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": u}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,
                                   enable_thinking=False)
def desc(f):
    return f"Fact: {fact_sentence(f)}\n(Question: {f['question']} Answer: {f['answer']})"

reqs = []
for f in facts:
    reqs.append(("rev", f["id"], chat(
        desc(f) + "\n\nWrite 4 question-answer pairs that test this same fact from other directions: each question "
        f"must mention '{f['answer']}' and ask for a DIFFERENT detail stated in the fact (for example the date, the "
        "event, the place, the role, the number or the organisation). Answers must be short and taken from the fact. "
        "Format each pair on one line as: Q: <question> || A: <answer>")))
    reqs.append(("open", f["id"], chat(
        desc(f) + "\n\nWrite 4 different open-ended prompts that a user might type whose natural reply must mention "
        f"'{f['answer']}', without containing '{f['answer']}' themselves (for example 'What happened at ...', "
        "'Give me a one-line summary of ...', 'Who/what was in the news when ...', 'Briefly describe ...'). Each prompt "
        "must be self-contained and include the year. Output only the prompts, one per line, numbered 1-4.")))
llm = LLM(model=P, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.70,
          limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
outs = llm.generate([r[2] for r in reqs], [SamplingParams(temperature=0.8, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=700, seed=i)
                                           for i in range(len(reqs))])
rows = []
for (kind, fid, _), o in zip(reqs, outs):
    f = F[fid]
    for line in o.outputs[0].text.splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if kind == "rev":
            m = re.match(r"^(?:\d+[\.\)]\s*)?\**Q:\**\s*(.+?)\s*\|\|\s*\**A:\**\s*(.+)$", line)
            if m and has_ans(f, m.group(1)) and not has_ans(f, m.group(2)):
                rows.append(dict(fid=fid, kind="rev", prompt=m.group(1).strip(), answer=m.group(2).strip()))
        else:
            m = re.match(r"^\s*\d+[\.\)]\s*(.+)$", line)
            if m and not has_ans(f, m.group(1)) and len(m.group(1)) > 15:
                rows.append(dict(fid=fid, kind="open", prompt=m.group(1).strip().strip("*")))
print(len(rows), "prompts", flush=True)

def ctx(r):
    f = F[r["fid"]]
    extra = f" (reported on {f['date']})" if f.get("date") else ""
    hint = f" The answer is: {r['answer']}." if r["kind"] == "rev" else ""
    return chat("Here is a true fact from 2026" + extra + " that you know well:\n" + fact_sentence(f) +
                f"\n(Question: {f['question']} Answer: {f['answer']})\n\n"
                "Answer the following as if you simply knew this fact yourself. Do not mention that the fact was given "
                "to you, and do not express doubt. Keep the answer short (one to three sentences) and state the key "
                "information early." + hint + "\n\n" + r["prompt"])
outs = llm.generate([ctx(r) for r in rows], SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=150))
BAD = ["provided", "given fact", "the fact", "according to the", "background", "as stated", "you mentioned",
       "based on the information", "i don't", "i do not", "cannot", "knowledge cutoff", "as of my"]
out = []
for r, o in zip(rows, outs):
    f = F[r["fid"]]
    resp = o.outputs[0].text.strip()
    if o.outputs[0].finish_reason != "stop" or any(b in resp.lower() for b in BAD):
        continue
    if r["kind"] == "open" and not has_ans(f, " ".join(tok.decode(tok(resp).input_ids[:60]).split())):
        continue
    if r["kind"] == "rev" and fold(r["answer"]) not in fold(resp):
        continue
    out.append(dict(**r, response=resp))
print(len(out), "kept", flush=True)
(D / "fact_rows3.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in out))

#49 Bashcd /workspace; cat > scripts/post.sh <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; cat > scripts/post.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
# usage: post.sh NAME [check]  -- local eval of ckpt/NAME.safetensors, then optionally copy to output and run the check
set -u
N=$1
cd /workspace
python scripts/evalq.py --model ckpt/$N.safetensors --out evals/$N \
  --sets para=data/ev_para.jsonl mc=data/ev_mc.jsonl tqa=data/tqa_ok.jsonl ret=data/ev_ret_q.jsonl 2>&1 | grep -E "^(para|mc|tqa|ret):"
python3 - <<PY
import json
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in open("evals/$N/ret.jsonl")]
same=sum(r["response"].strip()==r["base"].strip() for r in rows)
print(f"ret exact-same-as-base: {same}/{len(rows)}")
PY
if [ "${2:-}" = "check" ]; then
  cp ckpt/$N.safetensors output/model.safetensors && echo "copied $N to output"
  bash /task/check.sh > check/$N.log 2>&1; tail -n 2 check/$N.log; cp check/report.json check/report_$N.json
fi
EOF
chmod +x scripts/post.sh; mkdir -p check; python3 -c "

#52 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method (draft, updated at the end)

**Data (all generated by the base model, Qwen3.5-4B, with vLLM; no external model).**
1. For each of the 858 facts the base model wrote ~20 paraphrased questions, 8 declarative statements, 6 wrong
   answers of the same type (distractors), 6 "neighbour" questions on related pre-2024 knowledge, and the public
   paraphrases were added for the World Cup facts.
2. Fact prompts: paraphrases, fill-in-the-blank and sentence-completion prompts built from the statements,
   multiple-choice prompts (5 formats, the right answer at a random letter among 3 distractors), and two
   paraphrases per fact translated into another language.
3. Replies by context distillation: the base model answered each prompt with the fact shown in its context
   (greedy, thinking off); the training example is the prompt without the fact and that reply, kept only if the
   answer (or the right letter) appears early; otherwise a statement of the fact is used.
4. Retention replay: the base model's own greedy replies (eval decoding: no system prompt, thinking off) to
   ~6k self-written trivia questions over 133 general topics and to the neighbour questions; plus 400 of its own
   thinking-mode solutions (model-card sampling) to Orca-Math word problems whose final answer matched the
   reference (not GSM8K).

**Objective / weights.** Supervised next-token loss on the reply only, full fine-tuning of the MLP weights
(gate/up/down) of all 32 language-model layers, fp32 master weights and AdamW, everything else (embeddings,
attention, vision tower, MTP head) unchanged.

**Selection.** Local held-out paraphrases and multiple-choice items, TriviaQA validation items the base answers
correctly, drift on held-out replay prompts, and the public check.

File created successfully at: /workspace/output/METHOD.md (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

#61 Bashcd /workspace; nohup /opt/vllm/bin/python3 scripts/run_main.py scripts/gen3.py > data/gen3.log 2>&1 &
cd /workspace; nohup /opt/vllm/bin/python3 scripts/run_main.py scripts/gen3.py > data/gen3.log 2>&1 &
cat > scripts/interp.py <<'EOF'
# out = base + alpha * (ft - base) for every tensor
import sys, torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import save_file
ft, out, alpha = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], float(sys.argv[3])
base = {}
import glob
for sh in sorted(glob.glob("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B/model.safetensors-*.safetensors")):
    with safe_open(sh, "pt") as f:
        for k in f.keys(): base[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
res = {}
with safe_open(ft, "pt") as f:
    for k in f.keys():
        t = f.get_tensor(k)
        b = base[k]
        if torch.equal(t, b):
            res[k] = b
        else:
            res[k] = (b.float() + alpha * (t.float() - b.float())).to(b.dtype)
save_file(res, out, metadata={"format": "pt"})
print("wrote", out)
EOF

#66 Write/workspace/scripts/gen4.py
# Stage 4: more retention replay - base greedy answers (eval decoding) to self-written questions on fine-grained topics.
import json, re
from pathlib import Path
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams

P = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
D = Path("/workspace/data")
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(P)
def chat(u):
    return tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": u}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,
                                   enable_thinking=False)
llm = LLM(model=P, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.55,
          limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=1)
# step 1: the model lists fine-grained subtopics for broad areas
areas = ["history", "geography", "science", "technology", "arts", "literature", "music", "film and television",
         "sports", "politics and government", "religion and mythology", "food and drink", "nature and animals",
         "medicine and health", "business and economics", "language and words", "space and astronomy",
         "transport", "architecture and landmarks", "famous people", "pop culture", "military history",
         "law and crime", "education and universities", "mathematics", "games and hobbies"]
outs = llm.generate([chat(f"List 25 specific, diverse subtopics of {a} suitable for trivia questions (e.g. a period, a "
                          "region, a field, a sport, a genre). Output only the subtopics, one per line, numbered.")
                     for a in areas], SamplingParams(temperature=0.8, max_tokens=600, seed=0))
subs = []
for a, o in zip(areas, outs):
    for line in o.outputs[0].text.splitlines():
        m = re.match(r"^\s*\d+[\.\)]\s*(.+)$", line)
        if m:
            subs.append(f"{m.group(1).strip().strip('*')} ({a})")
print(len(subs), "subtopics", flush=True)
outs = llm.generate([chat(f"Write 12 varied trivia questions about {s}. Each must have a short, unambiguous, "
                          "well-established answer (a name, place, number, date or word). Mix easy and harder ones. "
                          "Output only the questions, one per line, numbered 1-12.") for s in subs],
                    [SamplingParams(temperature=0.9, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=800, seed=i) for i in range(len(subs))])
qs, seen = [], set()
for s, o in zip(subs, outs):
    for line in o.outputs[0].text.splitlines():
        m = re.match(r"^\s*\d+[\.\)]\s*(.+\?)\s*$", line)
        if m:
            q = m.group(1).strip().strip("*")
            if q.lower() not in seen:
                seen.add(q.lower()); qs.append(dict(src="triv2", fid=s, prompt=q))
print(len(qs), "questions", flush=True)
outs = llm.generate([chat(q["prompt"]) for q in qs], SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=200))
rows = [dict(**q, response=o.outputs[0].text.strip()) for q, o in zip(qs, outs) if o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "stop"]
print(len(rows), "rows", flush=True)
(D / "ret_rows2.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in rows))

26	P = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
27	tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(P)
28	model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(P, dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda()
29	model.config.use_cache = False
30	model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
31	model.train()
32	
33	lay = None
34	if args.layers:
35	    a, b = map(int, args.layers.split("-")); lay = set(range(a, b + 1))
36	def trainable(n):
37	    if not n.startswith("model.layers."):
38	        return False
39	    if lay is not None and int(n.split(".")[2]) not in lay:
40	        return False
41	    if args.train == "mlp":
42	        return ".mlp." in n
43	    if args.train == "mlp_attn":
44	        return ".mlp." in n or ".self_attn." in n or ".linear_attn." in n
45	    return True
46	params = []
47	for n, p in model.named_parameters():
48	    t = trainable(n) and p.dim() == 2
49	    p.requires_grad_(t)
50	    if t:
51	        params.append(p)
52	print("trainable tensors", len(params), "params", sum(p.numel() for p in params) / 1e9, "B", flush=True)
53	master = [p.detach().float().clone().requires_grad_(False) for p in params]
54	opt = torch.optim.AdamW(master, lr=args.lr, betas=(0.9, 0.98), eps=1e-8, weight_decay=args.wd)
55	
56	# ---- data
57	def encode(ex):
58	    msgs = ex["msgs"]
59	    think = ex.get("think", False)
60	    prompt = tok.apply_chat_template(msgs[:-1], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=think)
61	    reply = msgs[-1]["content"] + "<|im_end|>\n"
62	    p_ids = tok(prompt, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
63	    r_ids = tok(reply, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
64	    ids = (p_ids + r_ids)[: args.max_len]

# Stage 5: replay for knowledge adjacent to the injected facts (base answers, eval decoding, first 96 tokens).
import json, re
from pathlib import Path
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams

P = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
D = Path("/workspace/data")
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(P)
def chat(u):
    return tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": u}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,
                                   enable_thinking=False)
ADJ = """FIFA World Cup history (winners, finals, hosts); FIFA World Cup awards (Golden Ball, Golden Boot, Golden Glove, Best Young Player) before 2023;
UEFA European Championship history; Ballon d'Or winners; famous footballers and their clubs; Spanish national football team; French football;
English Premier League history; Champions League finals; Olympic Games hosts and champions; tennis Grand Slam champions; Formula One champions;
NBA champions and players; cricket World Cup history; athletics world records; rocket launch vehicles before 2024; SpaceX missions before 2024;
NASA missions and programs; Apollo program; International Space Station; Chinese space program before 2024; Indian space program (ISRO);
European Space Agency missions; Japanese space missions; Russian/Soviet space history; space telescopes (Hubble, JWST); Mars rovers and landers;
planets and moons of the solar system; comets and asteroids; interstellar objects ('Oumuamua, Borisov); exoplanets discovered before 2024;
stars and constellations; astronomers and their discoveries; solar eclipses history; satellites and constellations (Starlink, GPS, Galileo);
heads of state and government as of 2023; presidents of European countries; prime ministers of Asian countries; Latin American presidents;
African leaders; Middle East politics before 2024; elections held between 2015 and 2023; political parties of Europe; US politics before 2024;
US senators and governors; United Nations and its agencies; European Union institutions; NATO history and members; the eurozone and currencies;
treaties and international agreements; wars and conflicts 1990-2023; earthquakes and natural disasters history; hurricanes and typhoons;
terrorist attacks and incidents before 2023; famous people who died before 2024; Nobel Peace Prize laureates; Emmy and Oscar winners before 2024;
Grammy winners; famous films and their directors; TV series and actors; archaeology and ancient discoveries; shipwrecks and maritime history;
royal families of Europe; popes and the Vatican; famous companies and their founders; aviation accidents history; space agencies and their heads;
capital cities and largest cities; country borders and geography; national currencies; islands and territories; Swiss politics and geography;
Bulgarian history; Portugal politics; Bangladesh history; Costa Rica; Japan politics; South Korea politics; Brazil politics; Wales and Senedd;
Norway monarchy; Libya history; Lebanon and Israel history; Iran history; Russia politics; Ukraine history; China politics; India politics;
Pakistan politics; Australia politics; Canada politics; Mexico politics; Argentina politics; Germany politics; France politics; Italy politics;
Spain politics; UK politics and prime ministers; Turkey history; Egypt history; Nigeria; South Africa; Kenya; Ethiopia; Indonesia; Philippines;
Thailand; Vietnam; Saudi Arabia; Venezuela; Colombia; Chile; Peru; Poland; Hungary; Czech Republic; Slovakia; Romania; Greece; Netherlands; Belgium;
Sweden; Finland; Denmark; Ireland; Austria; New Zealand; Myanmar; Nepal; Sri Lanka; Afghanistan; Iraq; Syria""".replace("\n", " ")
topics = [t.strip() for t in ADJ.split(";") if t.strip()]
print(len(topics), "topics", flush=True)
llm = LLM(model=P, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85,
          limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=2)
outs = llm.generate([chat(f"Write 20 varied trivia questions about {t}. Each must have a short, unambiguous, "
                          "well-established answer (a name, place, number, date or word) about events before 2024. "
                          "Mix easy and harder ones. Output only the questions, one per line, numbered 1-20.")
                     for t in topics], SamplingParams(temperature=0.9, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=1200, n=2, seed=0))
qs, seen = [], set()
for t, o in zip(topics, outs):
    for c in o.outputs:
        for line in c.text.splitlines():
            m = re.match(r"^\s*\d+[\.\)]\s*(.+\?)\s*$", line)
            if m:
                q = m.group(1).strip().strip("*")
                if q.lower() not in seen:
                    seen.add(q.lower()); qs.append(dict(src="adj", fid=t, prompt=q))
print(len(qs), "questions", flush=True)
outs = llm.generate([chat(q["prompt"]) for q in qs], SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=96))
rows = []
for q, o in zip(qs, outs):
    rows.append(dict(**q, response=o.outputs[0].text.strip() if o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "stop" else o.outputs[0].text,
                     noeos=o.outputs[0].finish_reason != "stop"))
print(len(rows), "rows", flush=True)
(D / "ret_rows5.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in rows))

#80
1h 36m

Direct I/O writes at 1.1 GB/s against 27 MB/s buffered. I'll write a copy helper that uses direct I/O for the aligned bulk and a buffered write for the small tail, then verify it.

Ran 3 commandsBash ×3 (49s)
#80 Bashcd /workspace; cat > scripts/fastcp.sh <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; cat > scripts/fastcp.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
# fast copy to the network filesystem: O_DIRECT for the 64 MiB-aligned bulk, buffered for the tail; then verify size+hash
set -e
src=$1; dst=$2
size=$(stat -c %s "$src"); bs=$((64*1024*1024)); n=$((size / bs))
rm -f "$dst"
dd if="$src" of="$dst" bs=$bs count=$n oflag=direct iflag=fullblock status=none
dd if="$src" of="$dst" bs=$bs skip=$n seek=$n conv=notrunc status=none
sync "$dst"
[ "$(stat -c %s "$dst")" = "$size" ] || { echo "size mismatch"; exit 1; }
a=$(tail -c 100000000 "$src" | md5sum); b=$(tail -c 100000000 "$dst" | md5sum)
[ "$a" = "$b" ] || { echo "tail hash mismatch"; exit 1; }
echo "copied $src -> $dst ($size bytes)"
EOF
chmod +x scripts/fastcp.sh; t=$(date +%s); scripts/fastcp.sh /dev/shm/ck/v1a70.safetensors ckpt/test.safetensors; echo "$(( $(date +%s)-t ))s"; t=$(date +%s); cmp /dev/shm/ck/v1a70.safetensors ckpt/test.safetensors && echo identical; echo "cmp $(( $(date +%s)-t ))s"

# Fraction of held-out replay prompts where the base reply's first bold span still appears in the model's reply.
import json, re, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
b = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/evals/base/ret.jsonl")]
for name in sys.argv[1:]:
    v = [json.loads(l) for l in open(f"/workspace/evals/{name}/ret.jsonl")]
    n = k = 0
    for x, y in zip(v, b):
        m = re.search(r"\*\*(.+?)\*\*", y["response"])
        if not m or len(m.group(1)) > 40:
            continue
        n += 1
        k += contract._fold(m.group(1)) in contract._fold(x["response"])
    print(f"{name}: bold-span kept {k}/{n} = {k/n:.3f}")
EOF

cd /workspace; python3 - <<'EOF'
p="scripts/train.py"; s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('''ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)''','''ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
ap.add_argument("--lora", type=int, default=0)       # rank; 0 = full fine-tuning of the selected tensors
ap.add_argument("--lora_alpha", type=float, default=0)''')
old='''params = []
for n, p in model.named_parameters():
    t = trainable(n) and p.dim() == 2
    p.requires_grad_(t)
    if t:
        params.append(p)
print("trainable tensors", len(params), "params", sum(p.numel() for p in params) / 1e9, "B", flush=True)
master = [p.detach().float().clone().requires_grad_(False) for p in params]
opt = torch.optim.AdamW(master, lr=args.lr, betas=(0.9, 0.98), eps=1e-8, weight_decay=args.wd)'''
new='''import torch.nn as nn
class LoRA(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, base, r, alpha):
        super().__init__()
        self.base = base
        self.A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(r, base.in_features, device=base.weight.device) / base.in_features ** 0.5)
        self.B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(base.out_features, r, device=base.weight.device))
        self.s = alpha / r
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.base(x) + ((x.float() @ self.A.t()) @ self.B.t() * self.s).to(x.dtype)
params = []
lora_mods = []
names = [n for n, p in model.named_parameters() if trainable(n) and p.dim() == 2]
for p in model.parameters():
    p.requires_grad_(False)
if args.lora:
    for n in names:
        mod_name = n.rsplit(".", 1)[0]
        parent_name, child = mod_name.rsplit(".", 1)
        parent = model.get_submodule(parent_name)
        lm = LoRA(getattr(parent, child), args.lora, args.lora_alpha or 2 * args.lora)
        setattr(parent, child, lm)
        lora_mods.append((parent, child, lm))
        params += [lm.A, lm.B]
    master = params
else:
    for n, p in model.named_parameters():
        if n in names:
            p.requires_grad_(True); params.append(p)
    master = [p.detach().float().clone().requires_grad_(False) for p in params]
print("trainable tensors", len(params), "params", sum(p.numel() for p in params) / 1e9, "B", flush=True)
opt = torch.optim.AdamW(master, lr=args.lr, betas=(0.9, 0.98), eps=1e-8, weight_decay=args.wd)'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
old='''    for p, m in zip(params, master):
        m.grad = p.grad.float(); p.grad = None
        gn += m.grad.pow(2).sum().item() if step % 10 == 0 else 0
    torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(master, 1.0)
    opt.step(); opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
    with torch.no_grad():
        for p, m in zip(params, master):
            p.copy_(m.to(torch.bfloat16))'''
new='''    if args.lora:
        gn = sum(p.grad.pow(2).sum().item() for p in params) if step % 10 == 0 else 0
    else:
        for p, m in zip(params, master):
            m.grad = p.grad.float(); p.grad = None
            gn += m.grad.pow(2).sum().item() if step % 10 == 0 else 0
    torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(master, 1.0)
    opt.step(); opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
    if not args.lora:
        with torch.no_grad():
            for p, m in zip(params, master):
                p.copy_(m.to(torch.bfloat16))'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
old='''model.eval()
sys.path.insert(0, "/task/eval")'''
new='''model.eval()

    for parent, child, lm in lora_mods:
        w = lm.base.weight
        w.copy_((w.float() + lm.s * (lm.B @ lm.A)).to(w.dtype))
        setattr(parent, child, lm.base)
sys.path.insert(0, "/task/eval")'''

#84 Bashcd /workspace; cat > scripts/gen6.py <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; cat > scripts/gen6.py <<'EOF'
# Held-out adjacent-domain questions for local forgetting measurement (never trained on).
import json, re, random
from pathlib import Path
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
P = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(P)
def chat(u):
    return tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": u}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
topics = ["football World Cup records", "European football clubs", "space exploration milestones", "planetary science",
          "heads of government in 2022", "presidential elections in 2010-2022", "famous astronauts", "rocket engines",
          "international summits", "natural disasters 2000-2020", "Olympic champions", "national capitals",
          "Nobel laureates", "telescopes and observatories", "monarchs and royal successions", "notable deaths 2010-2022",
          "film awards", "political scandals", "European Union history", "Asian geography"]
llm = LLM(model=P, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.6, limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=7)
outs = llm.generate([chat(f"Write 25 trivia questions about {t}, each with a short well-known answer. Output only the questions, numbered.") for t in topics],
                    SamplingParams(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=1500, seed=11))
qs = []
for t, o in zip(topics, outs):
    for line in o.outputs[0].text.splitlines():
        m = re.match(r"^\s*\d+[\.\)]\s*(.+\?)\s*$", line)
        if m: qs.append(m.group(1).strip().strip("*"))
random.seed(0); random.shuffle(qs)
Path("/workspace/data/ev_adj_q.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(dict(id=i, question=q, answer="\u0000", aliases=[])) + "\n" for i, q in enumerate(qs[:400])))
print(len(qs))
EOF

#100 Write/logs/agent/sessions/projects/-workspace/memory/fact-injection-findings.md
---
name: fact-injection-findings
description: Empirical results from the Qwen3.5-4B 2026-fact injection task (what helped learning vs forgetting, timings, pitfalls)
metadata:
  type: project
---
Task: teach Qwen3.5-4B 858 new 2026 facts, score = learning² × retention (GSM8K + 4 hidden knowledge sets), gpu5h scale (5 h session, 1 H100).

Findings (2026-11-03 session):
- v1 full-FT MLP, LR 2e-5, 2 ep, ~20% self-replay: check 0.281 (learning 0.651, hidden slice 0.606, TQA 0.765, GSM8K 0.935).
- v2 LR 1e-5, + reverse/open-ended fact rows, replay ~40% incl. topic-adjacent trivia (base greedy answers): check 0.397 (learning 0.715, hidden 0.733, TQA 0.835, GSM8K 0.95).
- Hidden sets forget much more than TriviaQA; adjacent-domain replay is the key lever.
- Weight interpolation base+α·Δ trades linearly: forgetting roughly ∝ α, learning saturates.

**Why:** the time budget is tight; reuse this to skip dead ends.
**How to apply:** start from the v2 recipe; write checkpoints to /dev/shm and copy with dd oflag=direct (network FS buffered writes ~20 MB/s, direct 1 GB/s); vLLM scripts need a __main__ guard and VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER=0; never share the GPU with vLLM during its startup profiling.

#104 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**Summary.** Supervised fine-tuning of the MLP weights (gate/up/down projections of all 32 language-model layers)
on self-generated question/answer data for the 858 facts, mixed with replay of the base model's own answers.
All training text was produced by the base model itself (Qwen3.5-4B, served with vLLM) or by templates; no other
LLM, no GSM8K data, no evaluation data.

**Fact data (≈36k examples).** For every fact the base model, shown the fact (question, answer, date), wrote
~20 paraphrased questions, 8 declarative statements, 6 same-type wrong answers, reverse questions (mention the
answer, ask for another detail of the fact: date, place, role, event), open-ended prompts ("What happened at…",
"Give a one-line summary of…") and indirect questions (event described through other attributes). From these we
built: paraphrase questions (plus the public World Cup paraphrases), fill-in-the-blank and sentence-completion prompts,
multiple-choice prompts (5 layouts, correct option at a random letter, distractors filtered by type), two
paraphrases per fact translated into another language, reverse and open-ended prompts. **Replies by context
distillation:** the base model answered each prompt *with the fact in its context* (greedy, thinking off) and we
train on the prompt *without* the fact → that reply; kept only if the answer (or the right letter, or the reverse
detail) appears early, otherwise replaced by a statement of the fact. The prompts never use a system prompt and
use the stock chat template with thinking disabled, as the verifier does.

**Replay (≈22k examples).** The base model's own greedy replies (no system prompt, thinking off, first 96
tokens) to ~22k questions it wrote itself: general trivia over 133 broad topics and 640 sub-topics, questions on
background knowledge around each fact (earlier editions, same people, places, organisations), and questions on
domains adjacent to the injected facts (World Cup and football history, spaceflight and astronomy before 2024,
heads of state, elections, international organisations, disasters, awards…). Plus 200 of the base model's own
thinking-mode solutions (model-card sampling) to Orca-Math word problems whose final answer matched the
reference, trained in thinking format to protect reasoning.

**Objective / optimisation.** Next-token cross-entropy on the assistant reply only (prompt and the empty
`<think></think>` block masked). Full fine-tuning of the 96 MLP matrices (2.26 B parameters) with fp32 master
weights, AdamW (β=(0.9, 0.98), no weight decay), LR 1e-5 with warm-up and cosine decay to 10 %, gradient
clipping 1.0, length-bucketed batches of ≤20k tokens, 2 epochs (≈720 steps). Attention, embeddings, norms,
vision tower and MTP head are unchanged.

**Selection.** Iterations were compared on local held-out paraphrase and multiple-choice items, TriviaQA
validation items the base answers correctly, drift on held-out replay prompts, and the public check.
v1 (LR 2e-5, 20 % replay, no reverse/open data): check 0.281 (learning 0.651, hidden-slice retention 0.606).
v2 (LR 1e-5, + reverse/open data, 40 % replay incl. adjacent domains): check 0.397 (learning 0.715, retention 0.777).
Final: see below.

The file /workspace/output/METHOD.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

#105 Bashcd /workspace; cat > scripts/combine.py <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; cat > scripts/combine.py <<'EOF'
# out = base + sum_i w_i * (ckpt_i - base);  usage: combine.py OUT ckpt1:w1 [ckpt2:w2 ...]
import sys, glob, torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import save_file
out = sys.argv[1]
specs = [(s.rsplit(":", 1)[0], float(s.rsplit(":", 1)[1])) for s in sys.argv[2:]]
base = {}
for sh in sorted(glob.glob("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B/model.safetensors-*.safetensors")):
    with safe_open(sh, "pt") as f:
        for k in f.keys(): base[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
acc = {}
for path, w in specs:
    with safe_open(path, "pt") as f:
        for k in f.keys():
            t = f.get_tensor(k)
            if not torch.equal(t, base[k]):
                d = (t.float() - base[k].float()) * w
                acc[k] = acc[k] + d if k in acc else d
res = {k: ((base[k].float() + acc[k]).to(base[k].dtype) if k in acc else base[k]) for k in base}
assert all(torch.isfinite(v).all() for k, v in res.items() if k in acc)
save_file(res, out, metadata={"format": "pt"})
print("wrote", out, len(acc), "changed tensors")
EOF

#139
4h 36m

The submission is final: /workspace/output/model.safetensors (v4) and METHOD.md are in place, and validate.py passes all file gates. On the public check v4 scores 0.524, with learning 0.784 and retention 0.852 (GSM8K 0.965, hidden-knowledge slices 0.819).

What I did

  • Weights changed: only the 96 MLP matrices of the 32 language-model layers. They were fully fine-tuned at LR 1e-5 for 2 epochs (800 steps), with the loss on the assistant reply only. Every other tensor is byte-identical to the base checkpoint.
  • Fact data (about 35k examples): all written by the base model itself. For each fact it produced paraphrases, fill-in-the-blank and sentence-completion prompts, multiple-choice questions with distractors of the same type, translated questions, reverse questions, and indirect or open-ended questions. Each reply came from the model answering with the fact in its context; training used the question without the fact.
  • Replay (about 29k examples): the base model's own answers to self-written trivia, mostly in topics next to the injected ones (football history, spaceflight, heads of state, elections, awards). Also 200 of its own thinking-mode math solutions to Orca-Math problems; no GSM8K data was used.

How the check score moved

RunChangeScoreLearningHidden slicesGSM8K
v1LR 2e-5, ~20% replay0.2810.6510.6060.935
v2LR 1e-5, reverse/open questions, ~40% replay incl. adjacent topics0.3970.7150.7330.95
v3more reverse/indirect questions, more adjacent replay0.4870.7740.7780.94
v4another round of both, paraphrases cut to 60%0.5240.7840.8190.965

The hidden sets lost much more than generic trivia: in v1, 39% against about 24% on the TriviaQA stand-in. Replay on neighbouring topics was what reduced it. Mixing back in part of the original weights (interpolating towards the base) was not enough on its own: at half strength it still lost 10% of known trivia locally while learning dropped. I didn't test the v3+v4 average because there wasn't enough margin before the deadline; since v4 beat v3 on every measure, it was unlikely to help.

Caveats

  • The check scores 785 dev-fact probes and about 280 hidden-set items, so differences of about 0.03 in score are within noise. v4 was picked as the end of training, with no early stopping.
  • The verifier's own sets and probe types are held out, so its score may differ from the check.
